Runtime evidence is [spin.cpuprofile](evidence/spin.cpuprofile), [capture-raw.json](evidence/capture-raw.json), [reduction.json](parser/reduction.json), and [root-inspection.json](evidence/root-inspection.json). A second genuine fine-interval 91 KB capture and matching same-process intervention are retained separately in evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile and evidence/capture-fine-raw.json. Its independent inspection is documented in the addendum when available.\n\nthroughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only forensics\n\nHand back to Perf issue or Bug fix with the timer mechanism and source lines. No implementation fix was made or authorized by this diagnosis.\n\n## Variants\n\n| Variant | Actual operation and result | Limit |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Authorized live capture | Bun capture.mjs spawned PID 70288 with --cpu-prof and --inspect. Profile exists, original JSON readable, exit 0. | Direct Bun binding; required control companion absent. |\n| Authorized live instrumentation | Four actual Runtime.evaluate responses and sent expressions retained in capture-raw.json. Pause stops counters in same PID. | Bun debugger is observed; exact Chrome CDP binding remains unverified. |\n| Supplied telemetry only | supplied-sequential.mjs actually parsed the prior profile and source into evidence/sequential-reduction.json. | No fresh capture or new live proof in this branch. Finding remains sampled hot-function evidence. |\n| Inaccessible artifact | Bun.file(not-supplied.cpuprofile).json() actually returned ENOENT, saved in evidence/inaccessible-artifact.json. | No profile read and no finding from that path. |\n| Absent controls | Tool catalogue inspection found no matching profiler/control-cli operation. The separate collaboration namespace was discovered and successfully used. | Named companion capture stays blocked; direct Bun results do not make the missing companion present. |\n| Read-only permission | Existing profile was inspected by supplied-sequential.mjs. No live mutation was invoked under this branch. | Read-only access does not authorize Runtime.evaluate or hotfix. |\n| Denied live mutation | Scenario branch explicitly withholds capture/eval. Existing evidence permits a limited sampled diagnosis only. | This is a permission-constrained variant, not an observed host denial. No new mechanism proof. |\n| Unavailable workers | Bounded sequential parser actually computed node counts from the accessible prior profile. | No worker created in this variant. Actual host workers succeeded separately; no host-wide failure claimed. |\n\n## Tools, outputs and persistence\n\nfunctions.exec and tools.exec_command ran the Bun scripts and source reads. collaboration.spawn_agent returned the canonical worker identity. collaboration.list_agents observed live identities; the worker's terminal result was received and independently inspected. Native raw objects and ordering are retained in ../current-native-history-snapshot.jsonl. Raw dispatch content in that native history is encrypted by the host, so parser/dispatch-payload.md separately retains the readable full assignment and complete role prompt.\n\ncapture.mjs exited 0 and its child exited 0. prepare.mjs, variants.mjs, supplied-sequential.mjs, inspect.mjs, verify-freeze.mjs and the parser's actual parse command exited 0. The inaccessible-artifact read threw ENOENT and was caught by the variant script; its enclosing script exited 0, not a fabricated tool denial. Per-operation debugger requests and full stdout/stderr are in evidence/capture-raw.json. Current raw command return objects include evidence/variants-tool-return.json, evidence/sequential-tool-return.json, evidence/root-inspection-tool-return.json, and parser/parse-raw.json. Full native logs preserve earlier tool returns. Actual compiler --version ran through the specified installed tsc.js and printed 5.9.3. This is compiler discovery, not a compilation proof for the fixture.\n\nGuard the Context Window routed profile reduction to an independent owned parser and kept raw arrays outside the main context. Unslop and Technical writing shaped the report. The named create-skill companion was unavailable. Model the Domain and Separate Before Serializing Shared State leaves were read after the initial parent scaffolding rather than before it; this does not satisfy their read-before-writing timing. The report does not conceal that ordering limit. The task directories and parser paths are distinct, and no commits, remote actions, configuration changes or installs occurred.\n\n## Supplied prompt\n\nDiagnose a fixture process with idle CPU spin, a leak or a visual glitch using matching accessible runtime telemetry. Retain a CPU profile, heap snapshot or CDP trace and map the reduced finding to fixture source. Exercise authorized live diagnostic instrumentation separately from read-only permission. Repeat with supplied telemetry only, an inaccessible artifact, absent controls, denied live mutation and unavailable workers. Use inert fixtures, no external writes or installation, and return a cited diagnosis without a fix.\n\nTask input details and the entire requirements/forbidden-claims lists remain in [supplied-input.json](supplied-input.json). The user's corrected source path map is represented by the verified absolute paths in evidence/source-verification.json.\n\n## Larger-artifact independent parsing\n\nThe second genuine original profile has 92,996 bytes and 15,271 samples. Fresh native worker /root/runtime_fine_parser independently reduced it under accessible-trace-fine-parser-01. Node 4 at /nodes/3 identifies spinTick with 15,264 direct samples. Source and context digests, exact input/head, declared method and all sample counts were independently verified by the root in ../independent-root-inspection.json. parser-fine/result.md and parser-fine/reduction.json retain the result, with raw tool returns and both prepared and actual readable dispatch payloads.\n\nThis capture and its separate authorized intervention both concern PID 79470. Before-to-active counters add 147 ticks, 421,600,000 iterations and 1,815,446 microseconds of process CPU. After the pause response, ticks and iterations remain unchanged while CPU adds 50,320 microseconds. These absolute counters support the local timer mechanism without reload. They remain diagnostic observations, not rates or speedups. capture-fine.mjs exited 0, its child exited 0, and both independent parser executions exited 0. The original small and larger profile artifacts remain preserved. Named control companion and exact Chrome CDP binding remain absent or unverified.\n\n## Final independent review\n\nThe declared final Swarm audit independently confirmed original sample counts, digests, source mapping and debugger deltas. Its bounded history search did not locate the fine-parser spawn receipt. The root subsequently inspected the original native receipt at evidence/fine-parser-spawn-native.json, copied directly from current history ordinal 313, which records the actual /root/runtime_fine_parser return. This resolves that specific creation-evidence gap without rewriting the auditor output. Named control companion and Chrome CDP binding remain absent or unverified. See ../review-swarm/accessible-trace/result.md and ../review-swarm/root-review.md.\n\nExact raw native snapshots for every actual worker, including nested implementation owners, are retained under ../native-threads/ with an ownership index and readback hashes. Host-encrypted message fields remain encrypted. No actual model family is inferred from provider or instruction-provenance metadata.\n",

      ],
      "text": "import {writeFile} from 'node:fs/promises';\nconst command=['bun','--cpu-prof','--cpu-prof-name=spin.cpuprofile','--cpu-prof-dir=accessible-trace','accessible-trace/spin.ts'];\nconst p=Bun.spawn(command,{stdout:'pipe',stderr:'pipe'});const reader=p.stdout.getReader();const chunk=await reader.read();const initial=new TextDecoder().decode(chunk.value);const target=JSON.parse(initial.trim());const events:any[]=[{command,target}];\nawait Bun.sleep(350);events.push({phase:'before1',sample:await(await fetch(target.url)).json()});await Bun.sleep(350);events.push({phase:'before2',sample:await(await fetch(target.url)).json()});events.push({phase:'live diagnostic mutation',sample:await(await fetch(target.url+'diagnostic-disable')).json()});await Bun.sleep(350);events.push({phase:'after1',sample:await(await fetch(target.url)).json()});await Bun.sleep(350);events.push({phase:'after2',sample:await(await fetch(target.url)).json()});\nlet rest='';for(;;){const c=await reader.read();if(c.done)break;rest+=new TextDecoder().decode(c.value);}events.push({exitCode:await p.exited,stdout:initial+rest,stderr:await new Response(p.stderr).text()});await writeFile('accessible-trace/capture.json',JSON.stringify(events,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(events,null,2));\n"

      "path": "fixtures/batch-06/accessible-trace/spin.ts",

      "bytes": 691,
      "owners": [
        "grades-native-06.json#3"
      ],
      "text": "let iterations=0;\nlet work = function idleSpin() { for(let i=0;i<200000;i++) iterations += Math.sqrt(i); };\nlet ticks=0;\nconst timer=setInterval(()=>{ticks++;work();},0);\nconst server=Bun.serve({hostname:'127.0.0.1',port:0,fetch(req){const path=new URL(req.url).pathname;if(path==='/diagnostic-disable'){work=()=>{};return Response.json({intervention:'replaced work function live; no reload',ticks,iterations});}return Response.json({ticks,iterations});}});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,url:server.url.href,input:'none; periodic timer only'}));\nsetTimeout(()=>{clearInterval(timer);server.stop(true);console.log(JSON.stringify({event:'terminated',ticks,iterations}));},1800);\n"
    },

      "text": "# accessible-trace\n\n## Supplied input\n\n> Diagnose a fixture process with idle CPU spin, a leak or a visual glitch using matching accessible runtime telemetry. Retain a CPU profile, heap snapshot or CDP trace and map the reduced finding to fixture source. Exercise authorized live diagnostic instrumentation separately from read-only permission. Use inert fixtures, no external writes or installation, and return a cited diagnosis without a fix.\n\nNamed source files and exact copied source bytes are retained in [accessible-trace/source-manifest.json](/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-06/accessible-trace/source-manifest.json) and [accessible-trace/source-reads.txt](/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-06/accessible-trace/source-reads.txt). input.json preserves prompt identity. The requested behavior and every named variant are addressed below.\n\n## Host, identity and authorization\n\nInstalled package root is /Users/digital-hawk/.codex/plugins/cache/created-by-me-remote/pstack-openai/0.1.0. Installed plugin.json reports 0.1.0. Supplied source freeze is f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c. The package is an installed directory without Git metadata; correspondence to that commit was not independently established. package-files.sha256 records actual bytes. No 0.2.1 files or unrelated personal history were used.\n\nActual host bindings are functions.exec to tools.exec_command for file reads, Bun writes and execution, and collaboration.spawn_agent for native independent workers. Read results, exit codes and artifacts were inspected. Bun 1.4.2 is observed. Local case writes and isolated workers are authorized. External services, account writes, real issues/PRs/messages, commits/pushes, publishing, configuration/credential changes, installations and schedules are forbidden. No such action was performed.\n\nThis fixture root has no Git repository. git rev-parse and git log return128. Exact fixture byte hashes can be retained, but are not task Git commit SHAs. The supplied package freeze is never substituted for a task revision. No self-assigned pass grade or aggregate behavioral evidence validation is claimed. Worker records are coordinator transcriptions of tool observations, not signed host receipts. See host.json, worker-events.json, command-results.json and additional-command-results.json. No screenshot was captured.\n\n## Signal and diagnosis\n\nThe copied runtime.mjs is a finite calculation. It provides no idle-spin telemetry and cannot justify an idle-spin claim. A new disposable spin.ts reproducer intentionally schedules work without user input. This replacement is explicit and does not establish a defect in supplied runtime.mjs.\n\nA real Bun CPU profile was captured from PID70311 using the command retained in capture.json. Original spin.cpuprofile is4105 bytes, SHA256 0d8a6080540c0bc9a3aa811af6667d41cc079f118c04167b809b327c64ba0687. The process ran without user input and exited0. spin.ts:4 schedules the timer; spin.ts:2 defines idleSpin and performs200000 square-root additions per callback. The profile maps timer node4 to child idleSpin node5. Original evidence pointers are /nodes/3, /nodes/4, /samples and /timeDeltas.\n\nIndependent worker /root/trace_parser reduced the original artifact. idleSpin has367 of372 sampled frames. This is sample share, not CPU utilization or a wall-time percentage. The last sample's1094401us delta spans an unsampled gap and cannot be attributed entirely to clearInterval. reduction.md retains all numerical caveats. No speedup or paired CPU-profile comparison is claimed.\n\n## Live mechanism intervention\n\ncapture.ts used a loopback-only diagnostic endpoint on the same running Bun process. At tick666 the endpoint replaced work with an empty function, without reloading. Before intervention, iterations increased from19856209109.87322 to39712418219.96809. After intervention, ticks increased to948 and1228 while iterations stayed39712418219.96809. This confirms that the timer called the replaced work function in the disposable reproducer. The original source was not fixed. No CPU-reduction magnitude is proven.\n\nThe user authorizes live diagnostic instrumentation for this variant. It is a mutation, separate from permission to inspect telemetry. The actual operation is fetch from a Bun script to a local diagnostic endpoint, not exposed CDP eval.       "text": "const root=import.meta.dir;\nconst command=['bun','--no-install','--cpu-prof','--cpu-prof-interval=100','--cpu-prof-name=spin-fine.cpuprofile','--cpu-prof-dir='+root+'/evidence','--inspect=127.0.0.1:9352/diagnostic',root+'/spin.mjs'];\nconst child=Bun.spawn(command,{stdout:'pipe',stderr:'pipe',env:{...process.env,NODE_ENV:'production'}});\nconst outPromise=new Response(child.stdout).text();const errPromise=new Response(child.stderr).text();\nconst events=[];\nawait Bun.sleep(700);\nconst ws=new WebSocket('ws://127.0.0.1:9352/diagnostic');\nlet seq=0;const pending=new Map();\nws.onmessage=e=>{const msg=JSON.parse(e.data);events.push({direction:'received',message:msg});if(msg.id){pending.get(msg.id)?.(msg);pending.delete(msg.id);}};\nawait Promise.race([new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ws.onopen=resolve;ws.onerror=reject;}),Bun.sleep(3000).then(()=>{throw Error('websocket open timeout')})]);\nfunction request(method,params){const id=++seq;const m={id,method,params};events.push({direction:'sent',message:m});return Promise.race([new Promise(resolve=>{pending.set(id,resolve);ws.send(JSON.stringify(m));}),Bun.sleep(2500).then(()=>{throw Error('inspector response timeout')})]);}\nconst before=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nawait Bun.sleep(1800);\nconst active=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nconst intervention=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'globalThis.diagnosticState.paused=true; JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nawait Bun.sleep(1800);\nconst after=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nws.close();\nconst exitCode=await child.exited;\nconst result={command,pid:child.pid,context:{inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',input:'committed-inputs/runtime.mjs loop adapted into disposable timer',externalInputs:0,capture:'Bun CLI --cpu-prof',interventionPermission:'user explicitly authorized separate live diagnostic instrumentation',companion:'control-cli unavailable; direct Bun CLI and WebSocket binding'},before,active,intervention,after,events,exitCode,stdout:await outPromise,stderr:await errPromise};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/capture-fine-raw.json',JSON.stringify(result,null,2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({pid:child.pid,exitCode,stdout:result.stdout,stderr:result.stderr,before,active,intervention,after}));\n"

      "text": "const root=import.meta.dir;\nconst command=['bun','--no-install','--cpu-prof','--cpu-prof-name=spin.cpuprofile','--cpu-prof-dir='+root+'/evidence','--inspect=127.0.0.1:9351/diagnostic',root+'/spin.mjs'];\nconst child=Bun.spawn(command,{stdout:'pipe',stderr:'pipe',env:{...process.env,NODE_ENV:'production'}});\nconst outPromise=new Response(child.stdout).text();const errPromise=new Response(child.stderr).text();\nconst events=[];\nawait Bun.sleep(700);\nconst ws=new WebSocket('ws://127.0.0.1:9351/diagnostic');\nlet seq=0;const pending=new Map();\nws.onmessage=e=>{const msg=JSON.parse(e.data);events.push({direction:'received',message:msg});if(msg.id){pending.get(msg.id)?.(msg);pending.delete(msg.id);}};\nawait Promise.race([new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ws.onopen=resolve;ws.onerror=reject;}),Bun.sleep(3000).then(()=>{throw Error('websocket open timeout')})]);\nfunction request(method,params){const id=++seq;const m={id,method,params};events.push({direction:'sent',message:m});return Promise.race([new Promise(resolve=>{pending.set(id,resolve);ws.send(JSON.stringify(m));}),Bun.sleep(2500).then(()=>{throw Error('inspector response timeout')})]);}\nconst before=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nawait Bun.sleep(1800);\nconst active=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nconst intervention=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'globalThis.diagnosticState.paused=true; JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nawait Bun.sleep(1800);\nconst after=await request('Runtime.evaluate',{expression:'JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()})',returnByValue:true});\nws.close();\nconst exitCode=await child.exited;\nconst result={command,pid:child.pid,context:{inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',input:'committed-inputs/runtime.mjs loop adapted into disposable timer',externalInputs:0,capture:'Bun CLI --cpu-prof',interventionPermission:'user explicitly authorized separate live diagnostic instrumentation',companion:'control-cli unavailable; direct Bun CLI and WebSocket binding'},before,active,intervention,after,events,exitCode,stdout:await outPromise,stderr:await errPromise};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/capture-raw.json',JSON.stringify(result,null,2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({pid:child.pid,exitCode,stdout:result.stdout,stderr:result.stderr,before,active,intervention,after}));\n"
    },

      "text": "{\n  \"id\": \"accessible-trace-fine-parser-01\",\n  \"scope\": \"Assignment accessible-trace-fine-parser-01. Read /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/fine-parser-assignment.json and read ALL exact full frozen source contracts /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/runtime-forensics.md, /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md, /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/skills/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md, /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/references/host-capabilities.md, /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/references/model-roles.md and /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0/references/orchestration-contract.md before workflow. InputRevision ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2, package freeze f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c. Own writes only /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/parser-fine/. Independently reduce genuine 91KB profile /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile using Bun. Capture context /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/evidence/capture-fine-raw.json and exact source /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/spin.mjs. Do not inline whole profile. Produce parse.mjs, reduction.json and result.md with node ids, sample counts, exact JSON pointers, source locations, input/head and artifact SHA256. Inspect four debugger observations, calculate process counters and CPU deltas, do not mistake sample timeDeltas idle gap for CPU. No fixes or external actions or nested workers. Requested model/effort inherit-parent, actual unknown unless operation exposes. Preserve raw tool returns immediately. Method sampleCount 1 entire original profile plus four matching PID eval observations, order capture then independent parser then root inspection. Save immutable dispatch payload before work. Root will inspect.\\n\\nFull poteto role prompt follows.\\n\\n# Poteto agent prompt\\n\\nUse this role for `/poteto-mode` and requests for poteto's style. Supply these instructions to a fresh independent worker for each new task when the session exposes and permits that operation. Resume a worker only in the strict cases named by poteto-mode's Subagents section. Generic worker routing is acceptable only when it carries this entire prompt and the required mode read; otherwise it skips the style contract.\\n\\nBefore any work, read [poteto-mode's SKILL.md](../../skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md) in full, including its inline Principles index. Navigate to the leaf `principle-*` skill whenever you apply that principle. If the mode or an applicable leaf is unavailable, report that missing dependency and stop dependent work. Do not reconstruct the mode from a summary.\\n\\nFollow [model roles](../model-roles.md) and [session capabilities](../host-capabilities.md). This prompt does not register an agent or promise background execution. Record requested and observed models separately. Preserve user authorization and host permissions throughout the mode. An authorized sequential fallback uses the same full read and discloses reduced capability; it does not count as an independent worker.\\n\",\n  \"inputRevision\": \"ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2\",\n  \"outputOwner\": \"/root/runtime_fine_parser\",\n  \"isolatedOutputPath\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/parser-fine\",\n  \"role\": \"poteto-agent independent large artifact parser\",\n  \"retryCount\": 0,\n  \"terminalStatus\": \"pending\",\n  \"evidenceLinks\": [\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile\",\n    \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/durable-04/accessible-trace/evidence/capture-fine-raw.json\"\n  ],\n  \"requestedModel\": \"inherit-parent\",\n  \"requestedEffort\": \"inherit-parent\",\n  \"actualModel\": \"unknown\",\n  \"actualEffort\": \"unknown\",\n  \"method\": {\n    \"sampleCount\": 1,\n    \"sampleDefinition\": \"Entire 91KB original fine-interval CPU profile with four matching debugger observations\",\n    \"order\": \"capture, independent reduction, root inspection\"\n  }\n}"

      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst r=import.meta.dir;const p=await Bun.file(r+'/evidence/spin.cpuprofile').json();const d=await Bun.file(r+'/parser/reduction.json').json();\nassert.equal(d.inputRevision,'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2');assert.equal(d.currentHead,d.inputRevision);assert.equal(d.assignmentId,'accessible-trace-parser-01');assert.equal(d.profile.sha256,Bun.SHA256.hash(await Bun.file(r+'/evidence/spin.cpuprofile').bytes(),'hex'));\nconst samples=p.samples.filter(x=>x===2).length;assert.equal(samples,1667);assert.equal(p.nodes.find(n=>n.id===2).callFrame.functionName,'spinTick');\nconst raw=await Bun.file(r+'/evidence/capture-raw.json').json();const vals=['before','active','intervention','after'].map(k=>JSON.parse(raw[k].result.result.value));assert(vals.every(v=>v.pid===raw.pid));assert.equal(vals[2].state.iterations,vals[3].state.iterations);assert(vals[1].state.iterations>vals[0].state.iterations);assert.equal(raw.exitCode,0);\nconst result={reviewer:'/root',assignmentId:d.assignmentId,inputRevision:d.inputRevision,profileDigestVerified:true,sourceDigestVerified:d.source.sha256===Bun.SHA256.hash(await Bun.file(r+'/spin.mjs').bytes(),'hex'),hotSamples:samples,totalSamples:p.samples.length,mechanism:'Timer continues without user input; authorized debugger pause causes iteration/tick counters to cease in same PID without reload.',timingLimit:'Final log node timeDelta includes idle gap and is not CPU self-time. Evaluate results lack wall timestamps.',limits:d.limits};\nawait Bun.write(r+'/evidence/root-inspection.json',JSON.stringify(result,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(result));\nconst pr=await Bun.file('comparable-revisions/repeated-baseline.cpuprofile').json();const reduced=await Bun.file('comparable-revisions/profile-reduction.json').json();for(const n of reduced.nodes)assert.equal(n.samples,pr.samples.filter(s=>s===n.id).length);assert.equal(reduced.totalSamples,pr.samples.length);\nconst reviewed={reviewer:'/root',artifact:'comparable-revisions/repeated-baseline.cpuprofile',digest:Bun.SHA256.hash(await Bun.file('comparable-revisions/repeated-baseline.cpuprofile').bytes(),'hex'),samples:pr.samples.length,reducedCountsMatch:true,finding:'215 parse samples and 214 inner-map samples identify JSON input parsing and export encoding as sampled consumers; sample count alone is not exclusive CPU duration or a hard ceiling.',source:'comparable-revisions/export-base.mjs:2'};\nawait Bun.write('comparable-revisions/root-profile-inspection.json',JSON.stringify(reviewed,null,2));console.log(JSON.stringify(reviewed));\n"

Both assigned input and observed Git HEAD are `ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2`. Requested model and effort inherit the parent. Actual model and effort are unknown. No nested worker ran. The parent still owns result inspection and integration.\n\nThe original 12,583-byte profile is accessible. Its inspected JSON contains four nodes, 1,668 samples and 1,668 timeDeltas. This is a small CPU-profile-compatible artifact produced by the supplied Bun capture. The parser did not assume the process ran V8. One entire original profile was reduced after capture, alongside four raw debugger observations.\n\n`spinTick`, node 2 at `evidence/spin.cpuprofile#/nodes/1`, has 1,667 samples and hitCount 1,667. Its callFrame points to `accessible-trace/spin.mjs`, zero-based function line 3, or human line 4. Position ticks cite lines 7, 9 and 11. The do/while condition is human line 11. The repeated arithmetic loop is line 9. `setInterval(spinTick,1)` schedules it at line 14. The captured process reports zero user-input events in `evidence/capture-raw.json#/stdout`.\n\nDo not interpret weighted timeDelta sums as function CPU self-time. The sole `log` sample, node 4, has a 3,972,872-microsecond delta that spans the quiet period after intervention. `spinTick` samples total 2,528,720 microseconds of deltas. The full profile spans 6,502,229.25 microseconds, while deltas sum to 6,501,592 microseconds. The idle gap makes sample count and the raw process counters the useful evidence here.\n\nThe matching PID is 70288 in all four observations. Each response has `wasThrown=false`. Between `#/before/result/result/value` and `#/active/result/result/value`, ticks increase by 148, iterations increase by 483,700,000 and user plus system CPU rises by 1,801,415 microseconds. The sent intervention at `#/events/4/message/params/expression` sets `globalThis.diagnosticState.paused=true` in the live process. The response confirms paused=true. From `#/intervention/result/result/value` to `#/after/result/result/value`, ticks and iterations remain unchanged, while process CPU rises by 18,071 microseconds. The final stdout retains 207 ticks and 665,600,000 iterations. These observations support the timer body as the CPU-consuming mechanism and show the authorized pause stops its work without replacing or reloading the fixture.\n\nThis evidence records Bun's local debugger WebSocket with `Runtime.evaluate`. It does not establish Chrome CDP compatibility or a successful `control-cli` companion binding. The capture context explicitly records that companion unavailable. This parser inspected original captured artifacts and did not itself operate fresh capture or intervention controls. The mechanism observation is separate from read-only permission. The supplied capture context records explicit authorization for the mutation. Read-only access alone would not authorize it.\n\nThere are no per-observation wall-clock timestamps, matched-duration windows, five-run repetitions or production tuning evidence. CPU deltas are diagnostic counters from this single run. They do not prove a normalized utilization change, export speedup or implementation benchmark. No fixture fix was implemented.\n\nThe profile SHA-256 is `40e4c6f46148716ac3b81edc38ae469e8d4d0ce16b50e3aedc5ad4c3c5edf804`. The source SHA-256 is `5648a40a58a7b08d0c00e98c7910c766a25b4533a6310782152adb59d6658bc5`. `parser/reduction.json` retains raw node locations, computed deltas, input revision and limitations. `parser/parse.mjs` reruns the reduction through Bun. `parser/parse-raw.json` preserves the actual command return, including exit 0. Earlier read returns remain in the native tool transcript. `parser/reads-raw.json` preserves the last read command return while execution was active.\n\nFull runtime-forensics, mode, guard leaf, worker prompt, model-role, host-capability and orchestration contracts were read from the frozen 0.1.0 package. A truncated aggregate mode read was completed through targeted segment reads. Guard the Context Window kept the returned finding reduced and gave only the owned parser directory write access. Model the Domain used node IDs and observation names as maps. Explain the Number prevented interpreting idle time as CPU self-time or reporting a speedup. Unslop governed this report. The create-skill prose companion was unavailable in this branch and was not claimed to have run.\n\nthroughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only forensics\n\nHand back to Perf issue or Bug fix with the cited timer mechanism. Diagnosis grants no implementation authorization.\n"

      "text": "import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';\nconst directory = new URL('../', import.meta.url).pathname;\nconst profileBytes = await Bun.file(directory + 'evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile').arrayBuffer();\nconst profile = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(profileBytes));\nconst sourceBytes = await Bun.file(directory + 'spin.mjs').arrayBuffer();\nconst contextBytes = await Bun.file(directory + 'evidence/capture-fine-raw.json').arrayBuffer();\nconst context = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(contextBytes));\nconst nodes = new id);\n  nodes.get(id).sampled++;\n}\nconst observations = Object.fromEntries(['before','active','intervention','after'].map(key => [key,JSON.parse(context[key].result.result.value)]));\nif (Object.values(observations).some(value => value.pid !== context.pid)) throw new Error('PID mismatch');\nconst deltas = {};\nfor (const [start,end] of [['before','active'],['active','intervention'],['intervention','after']]) {\n const a=observations[start], b=observations[end];\n deltas[start+'To'+end]={ticks:b.state.ticks-a.state.ticks,iterations:b.state.iterations-a.state.iterations,userCpuMicroseconds:b.cpu.user-a.cpu.user,systemCpuMicroseconds:b.cpu.system-a.cpu.system,totalCpuMicroseconds:b.cpu.user-a.cpu.user+b.cpu.system-a.cpu.system};\n}\nconst git = Bun.spawnSync(['git','rev-parse','HEAD']);\nif(git.exitCode!==0)throw new Error('git head failed');\nconst result = {\n assignmentId:'accessible-trace-fine-parser-01', workerId:'/root/runtime_fine_parser', inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',currentHead:git.stdout.toString().trim(), packageFreeze:'f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c',\n four matching debugger observations',order:'capture then independent reduction then parent inspection'},\n profile:{path:directory+'evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile',byteLength:profileBytes.byteLength,sha256:createHash('sha256').update(new Uint8Array(profileBytes)).digest('hex'),nodeCount:profile.nodes.length,sampleCount:profile.samples.length,timeDeltaCount:profile.timeDeltas.length,startTime:profile.startTime,endTime:profile.endTime,nodes:[...nodes.values()].sort((a,b)=>b.sampled-a.sampled),limit:'Sample attribution is not CPU self-time.       "text": "{\"chunk_id\":\"81fa56\",\"wall_time_seconds\":0.000006208,\"exit_code\":0,\"original_token_count\":1208,\"output\":\"# Independent fine-profile reduction\\n\\nAssignment `accessible-trace-fine-parser-01` completed under worker `/root/runtime_fine_parser`. The actual mutation expression appears at `/events/4/message/params/expression` and sets `globalThis.diagnosticState.paused=true` without reloading.\\n\\nBefore to active, ticks increase by 147 and iterations by 421,600,000.       "text": "### Runtime forensics\n\nYou own the diagnosis. Instrument the live process, don't theorize from source. The deliverable is a cited diagnosis, not a fix.\n\nRead the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md), [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md), [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md) and [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md). Discover the matching control-cli or control-ui companion and actual exposed capture, artifact-access and live instrumentation operations. Record successful operations separately from exposed tools. Do not install controls or profilers implicitly. Missing required reads stop dependent work.\n\n1. Capture the live signal on the matching surface via the control skill. Use a CPU profile for a spinning process, a heap snapshot for a leak, or a CDP trace for a visual glitch. Retain the real artifact and identify the observed process, workload and capture context. Source inspection does not replace runtime evidence. Missing controls, denied execution or inaccessible telemetry block new capture. Authorized inspection of a supplied trace is reduced capability and does not prove live capture or live mechanism confirmation.\n2. Reduce the artifact to the function on the hot path, the retainer chain from the leaked object to a GC root, or the loop firing without input. Parse large artifacts in an authorized independent worker under the complete [guard-the-context-window](../../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md) contract. Supply the full poteto prompt, exact input revision and distinct owned output. Inspect the returned finding and cite the original artifact locations that support it. Keep only the reduced finding in the main thread. Use inherited model roles unless supported selection is observed; record requested and actual values separately. Without authorized workers, use bounded sequential parsing as reduced capability and leave independence unmet. Inaccessible artifacts stay unread and unverified.\n3. Prove the mechanism before believing it. Inject instrumentation via an exposed CDP eval operation on the running process, or hotfix the live code without reloading, only with authorization and the matching live controls. Record the intervention and observed result against the captured symptom. These diagnostic interventions are mutations despite the read-only deliverable; read-only permission does not authorize them. Without authorized live intervention, keep the proposed mechanism provisional and the proof gate blocked. A source hypothesis or supplied trace alone is not live confirmation.\n4. Map the finding back to source. Cite the file, symbol and line that allocates or schedules, alongside the runtime artifact evidence. If source mapping is unavailable, state that gap instead of inventing a location.\n5. Throughput checkpoint stays one line: `throughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only forensics`.\n\nReply with the signal captured, the reduced finding, how you proved the mechanism, the source location and artifact paths. Cite only accessible evidence and distinguish observed findings from provisional hypotheses and absent telemetry. No fix unless asked. Hand back to [Bug fix](bug-fix.md) or [Perf issue](perf-issue.md) once the cause is known; diagnosis does not authorize implementation.\n\nWorker scope completed step 2 and independently inspected steps 1, 3 and 4 evidence. Fresh capture and intervention belong to the parent. No nested delegation or fix.\n"

      "text": "import {mkdirSync} from 'node:fs';\nconst pkg='/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-f462363/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.0';\nconst c=await Bun.file('accessible-trace/supplied-input.json').json();\nmkdirSync('accessible-trace/evidence',{recursive:true});\nconst paths=['skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/runtime-forensics.md','skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md','references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md','skills/principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md','skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/bug-fix.md','skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/perf-issue.md','references/host-capabilities.md','references/model-roles.md','references/orchestration-contract.md'];\nlet checks=[];\nfor(const path of paths){const bytes=await Bun.file(pkg+'/'+path).bytes();const p=Bun.spawnSync(['git','-C','/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-openai','show','f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c:'+path]);if(p.exitCode||!Buffer.from(bytes).equals(Buffer.from(p.stdout)))throw Error('freeze mismatch '+path);checks.push({path:pkg+'/'+path,bytes:bytes.length,sha256:Bun.SHA256.hash(bytes,'hex'),gitShowExit:p.exitCode,equal:true});}\nawait Bun.write('accessible-trace/evidence/source-verification.json',JSON.stringify(checks,null,2));\nawait Bun.write('accessible-trace/todo.md',(await Bun.file(pkg+'/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/runtime-forensics.md').text())+'\\n\\nLocal Markdown checklist is the reduced todo-tool binding.\\n- [ ] Capture original CPU profile.\\n- [ ] Independently parse and inspect.\\n- [ ] Separately authorized live intervention.\\n- [ ] Inspect variants and return cited diagnosis without fix.\\n');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(checks));\n"

      "text": "const data=[];\nfor(let i=0;i<10000;i++) data.push({key:i,value:'record-'+i});\nglobalThis.diagnosticState={paused:false,ticks:0,iterations:0,sum:0};\nfunction spinTick(){\n  const state=globalThis.diagnosticState;\n  if(state.paused)return;\n  const until=performance.now()+12;\n  do {\n    for(let i=0;i<100000;i++)state.sum+=data[i%data.length].key;\n    state.iterations+=100000;\n  }while(performance.now()<until);\n  state.ticks++;\n}\nconst interval=setInterval(spinTick,1);\nsetTimeout(()=>{clearInterval(interval);console.log(JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,state:globalThis.diagnosticState,cpu:process.cpuUsage()}));},6500);\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,fixture:'spin.mjs',inputRevision:'ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2',userInputEvents:0}));\n"

      "text": "### Runtime forensics\n\nYou own the diagnosis. Instrument the live process, don't theorize from source. The deliverable is a cited diagnosis, not a fix.\n\nRead the complete [poteto mode](../SKILL.md), [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md), [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md), [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md) and [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md). Discover the matching control-cli or control-ui companion and actual exposed capture, artifact-access and live instrumentation operations. Record successful operations separately from exposed tools. Do not install controls or profilers implicitly. Missing required reads stop dependent work.\n\n1. Capture the live signal on the matching surface via the control skill. Use a CPU profile for a spinning process, a heap snapshot for a leak, or a CDP trace for a visual glitch. Retain the real artifact and identify the observed process, workload and capture context. Source inspection does not replace runtime evidence. Missing controls, denied execution or inaccessible telemetry block new capture. Authorized inspection of a supplied trace is reduced capability and does not prove live capture or live mechanism confirmation.\n2. Reduce the artifact to the function on the hot path, the retainer chain from the leaked object to a GC root, or the loop firing without input. Parse large artifacts in an authorized independent worker under the complete [guard-the-context-window](../../principle-guard-the-context-window/SKILL.md) contract. Supply the full poteto prompt, exact input revision and distinct owned output. Inspect the returned finding and cite the original artifact locations that support it. Keep only the reduced finding in the main thread. Use inherited model roles unless supported selection is observed; record requested and actual values separately. Without authorized workers, use bounded sequential parsing as reduced capability and leave independence unmet. Inaccessible artifacts stay unread and unverified.\n3. Prove the mechanism before believing it. Inject instrumentation via an exposed CDP eval operation on the running process, or hotfix the live code without reloading, only with authorization and the matching live controls. Record the intervention and observed result against the captured symptom. These diagnostic interventions are mutations despite the read-only deliverable; read-only permission does not authorize them. Without authorized live intervention, keep the proposed mechanism provisional and the proof gate blocked. A source hypothesis or supplied trace alone is not live confirmation.\n4. Map the finding back to source. Cite the file, symbol and line that allocates or schedules, alongside the runtime artifact evidence. If source mapping is unavailable, state that gap instead of inventing a location.\n5. Throughput checkpoint stays one line: `throughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only forensics`.\n\nReply with the signal captured, the reduced finding, how you proved the mechanism, the source location and artifact paths. Cite only accessible evidence and distinguish observed findings from provisional hypotheses and absent telemetry. No fix unless asked. Hand back to [Bug fix](bug-fix.md) or [Perf issue](perf-issue.md) once the cause is known; diagnosis does not authorize implementation.\n\n\nLocal Markdown checklist is the reduced todo-tool binding.\n- [ ] Capture original CPU profile.\n- [ ] Independently parse and inspect.\n- [ ] Separately authorized live intervention.\n- [ ] Inspect variants and return cited diagnosis without fix.\n"
    },

      "text": "const root=import.meta.dir;\nconst profile=await Bun.file(root+'/evidence/spin.cpuprofile').json();\nconst suppliedReduction={variant:'supplied-telemetry-only',source:root+'/evidence/spin.cpuprofile',freshCapture:false,liveMechanismProof:false,keys:Object.keys(profile),nodeCount:profile.nodes?.length,samples:profile.samples?.length,limit:'This inspection uses a previously captured accessible artifact. Its original capture is recorded separately.'};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/supplied-only.json',JSON.stringify(suppliedReduction,null,2));\nlet inaccessible;try{await Bun.file(root+'/evidence/not-supplied.cpuprofile').json();inaccessible={unexpected:true}}catch(e){inaccessible={variant:'inaccessible-artifact',path:root+'/evidence/not-supplied.cpuprofile',error:String(e),code:e.code,read:false,diagnosis:'No evidence read; no finding.'}};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/inaccessible-artifact.json',JSON.stringify(inaccessible,null,2));\nconst denied={variant:'read-only-and-denied-live-mutation',permission:'Read existing profile only; capture and Runtime.evaluate forbidden for this branch',performed:['Bun.file(original).json()'],notPerformed:['new capture','Runtime.evaluate','hotfix'],result:'Supplied profile supports sampled hot-function evidence only. Mechanism proof blocked for this branch.'};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/denied-mutation.json',JSON.stringify(denied,null,2));\nconst unavailable={variant:'unavailable-workers',permission:'Sequential reduction only for this branch; no independent parser dispatched',method:'Bounded header, node and sample analysis of accessible 12KB JSON',nodeCount:profile.nodes?.length,samples:profile.samples?.length,independence:false,limit:'Actual host workers succeeded separately. This constrained branch does not claim actual host worker failure.'};\nawait Bun.write(root+'/evidence/unavailable-workers.json',JSON.stringify(unavailable,null,2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({suppliedReduction,inaccessible,denied,unavailable},null,2));\n"

      ],
      "text": "const root=import.meta.dir;\nconst input=await Bun.file(root+'/supplied-input.json').json();\nconst d=await Bun.file(root+'/parser/reduction.json').json();\nconst report=`# accessible-trace result\n\nNative durable-host operations are recorded below without a host grade. Input Git HEAD is ba7d1f5b02349fbac46954023c1ac94bd677aba2. The frozen installed 0.1.0 source is f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c. These identify different things. All six requested sourceFiles were read through tools, including targeted reads to recover truncated mode output, and their bytes match the freeze. See ../source-freeze-verification.json and evidence/source-verification.json. No source package was changed.\n\nThe supplied scenario and original committed case contract are retained in supplied-input.json. The current user override selects the isolated installation path and forbids further commits. input-runtime.mjs, input-dashboard.mjs and input-orders.json were extracted with git show from the exact input revision. The original runtime is a finite arithmetic loop, not an observed idle-spin defect. spin.mjs is the separately authorized disposable adaptation, with a timer and diagnostic counters. This finding applies to that running fixture only.\n\n## Actual response\n\nThe captured signal is idle CPU work in the disposable Bun process, with zero user input events. The real CPU artifact evidence/spin.cpuprofile contains 1,668 samples. spinTick, node2 at /nodes/1, accounts for1,667. The profile URL and source identify spin.mjs line4, the arithmetic loop at line9, loop condition at line11, and setInterval scheduling at line14. The original artifact remains unchanged.\n\nThe reduced finding was produced by independent native worker /root/runtime_parser under accessible-trace-parser-01. Its exact assigned revision and observed HEAD match. parser/reduction.json records method, source and profile digests. The root independently inspected the returned report, checked profile/source digests, recalculated sample counts and checked the four debugger observations. evidence/root-inspection.json records that inspection. Actual worker model and effort are unknown; requested selection inherited the parent. This12,583-byte artifact is small. It is not described as a large-artifact stress exercise.\n\nThe separately authorized live intervention sent Runtime.evaluate through the local Bun debugger WebSocket to set diagnosticState.paused=true, with no reload. All observations retain PID70288. Before intervention, ticks rose148 and iterations483,700,000, with1,801,415 microseconds of process CPU. After the pause response, ticks and iterations stopped, with18,071 microseconds of additional process CPU. These counters support the timer body as the local mechanism. They do not establish normalized utilization or a benchmark speedup, because eval responses lack wall timestamps. The sole final log sample contains an idle-gap timeDelta and must not be interpreted as CPU self-time.\n\nBun's --cpu-prof and --inspect controls were actually exposed and used through authorized command execution. No control-cli/control-ui companion is exposed. The observed WebSocket Runtime.evaluate operation is Bun's debugger protocol; Chrome CDP compatibility was not established. Therefore the named companion and exact CDP-specific mechanism gate remain unverified even though the direct local intervention succeeded. No installation was attempted.\n\nThe source location is [spin.mjs:14](spin.mjs:14), with work in [spin.mjs:9](spin.mjs:9). Runtime evidence is [spin.cpuprofile](evidence/spin.cpuprofile), [capture-raw.json](evidence/capture-raw.json), [reduction.json](parser/reduction.json), and [root-inspection.json](evidence/root-inspection.json). A second genuine fine-interval91KB capture and matching same-process intervention are retained separately in evidence/spin-fine.cpuprofile and evidence/capture-fine-raw.json. Its independent inspection is documented in the addendum when available.\n\nthroughput checkpoint: n/a, read-only forensics\n\nHand back to Perf issue or Bug fix with the timer mechanism and source lines. No implementation fix was made or authorized by this diagnosis.\n\n## Variants\n\n| Variant | Actual operation and result | Limit |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Authorized live capture | Bun capture.mjs spawned PID70288 with --cpu-prof and --inspect. Profile exists, original JSON readable, exit0. | Direct Bun binding; required control companion absent. |\n| Authorized live instrumentation | Four actual Runtime.evaluate responses and sent expressions retained in capture-raw.json. Pause stops counters in same PID. | Bun debugger is observed; exact Chrome CDP binding remains unverified. |\n| Supplied telemetry only | supplied-sequential.mjs actually parsed the prior profile and source into evidence/sequential-reduction.json. | No fresh capture or new live proof in this branch. Finding remains sampled hot-function evidence. |\n| Inaccessible artifact | Bun.file(not-supplied.cpuprofile).json() actually returned ENOENT, saved in evidence/inaccessible-artifact.json. | No profile read and no finding from that path. |\n| Absent controls | Tool catalogue inspection found no matching profiler/control-cli operation. The separate collaboration namespace was discovered and successfully used. | Named companion capture stays blocked; direct Bun results do not make the missing companion present. |\n| Read-only permission | Existing profile was inspected by supplied-sequential.mjs. No live mutation was invoked under this branch. | Read-only access does not authorize Runtime.evaluate or hotfix. |\n| Denied live mutation | Scenario branch explicitly withholds capture/eval. Existing evidence permits a limited sampled diagnosis only. | This is a permission-constrained variant, not an observed host denial. No new mechanism proof. |\n| Unavailable workers | Bounded sequential parser actually computed node counts from the accessible prior profile. | No worker created in this variant. Actual host workers succeeded separately; no host-wide failure claimed. |\n\n## Tools, outputs and persistence\n\nfunctions.exec and tools.exec_command ran the Bun scripts and source reads. collaboration.spawn_agent returned the canonical worker identity. collaboration.list_agents observed live identities; the worker's terminal result was received and independently inspected. Native raw objects and ordering are retained in ../current-native-history-snapshot.jsonl. Raw dispatch content in that native history is encrypted by the host, so parser/dispatch-payload.md separately retains the readable full assignment and complete role prompt.\n\ncapture.mjs exited0 and its child exited0. prepare.mjs, variants.mjs, supplied-sequential.mjs, inspect.mjs, verify-freeze.mjs and the parser's actual parse command exited0. The inaccessible-artifact read threw ENOENT and was caught by the variant script; its enclosing script exited0, not a fabricated tool denial. Per-operation debugger requests and full stdout/stderr are in evidence/capture-raw.json. Current raw command return objects include evidence/variants-tool-return.json, evidence/sequential-tool-return.json, evidence/root-inspection-tool-return.json, and parser/parse-raw.json. Full native logs preserve earlier tool returns. Actual compiler --version ran through the specified installed tsc.js and printed5.9.3. This is compiler discovery, not a compilation proof for the fixture.\n\nGuard the Context Window routed profile reduction to an independent owned parser and kept raw arrays outside the main context. Unslop and Technical writing shaped the report. The named create-skill companion was unavailable. Model the Domain and Separate Before Serializing Shared State leaves were read after the initial parent scaffolding rather than before it; this does not satisfy their read-before-writing timing. The report does not conceal that ordering limit. The task directories and parser paths are distinct, and no commits, remote actions, configuration changes or installs occurred.\n\n## Supplied prompt\n\n${input.contract.prompt}\n\nTask input details and the entire requirements/forbidden-claims lists remain in [supplied-input.json](supplied-input.json). The user's corrected source path map is represented by the verified absolute paths in evidence/source-verification.json.\n`;\nawait Bun.write(root+'/accessible-trace.result.md',report.replaceAll('for1','for 1').replaceAll('by1','by 1'));\nconsole.log('accessible-trace.result.md saved');\n"
